- Remaining gaps (why CNT-01 stays Partial): categories carry no media or SEO fields, there is no drag-and-drop reordering, no tag management, no bulk move, and the storefront has no category landing pages yet (WEB-02). Drag-and-drop ordering, tag management and content keywords were closed on 2026-11-10 (records below); media fields on a category are still open. The legacy-URL redirect map landed the same day as an editor surface (P3.g record below), and a published record's slug change now writes its own 301 (record at the end).
- Gates: `tm-cms` `typecheck` and `lint` pass, `validate:build:cms` + `build:cms` succeed, and `test:content` (26 checks) still passes.

### CNT-01 / WEB-01 / WEB-02 — P3.c revisions and P3.d public listing record (2026-11-07)

- Target files: `tm-cms/server/models/revision.ts` (`content_revisions`, unique `(siteId, contentId, revision)`), `tm-cms/server/services/contentRevisions.ts` (`captureContentRevision`, `listContentRevisions`, `getContentRevision`, `revisionSnapshotOf`, `diffRevisionSnapshot`), `tm-cms/server/services/contentService.ts` (`ContentWriteMeta`, `restoreContentRevision`), `tm-cms/server/api/v1/admin/content/[id]/revisions{,.get,[revision].get,[revision]/restore.post}.ts`, migration `content-revision-indexes` v3, `tm-cms/server/utils/modelRegistry.ts` (`Revision`), `tm-cms/server/utils/contentApi.ts` (`writeMetaOf`, `revisionParamOf`, `ContentRestoreSchema`).
- Contracts: `AdminContentRevisionSchema`, `AdminContentRevisionDetailSchema`, `AdminContentRevisionDiffSchema`, `AdminContentRevisionSnapshotSchema`, audit action `revert`; public listing `PublicContentListItemSchema`, `PublicContentListSchema`.
- Behaviour: every create/update/event/restore captures one immutable snapshot of the editable fields in the same request (the unique index makes a retried command a no-op instead of a fork). Restoring `:revision` rewrites those fields as `revision + 1`, records audit action `revert` with the changed fields, leaves status/`publishedAt`/`deletedAt` untouched, and rejects a stale editor (`409`), the current revision (`422`) and an identical snapshot (`422`). Snapshots start at the revision the feature shipped; a record created before it has no backfilled snapshot until its next edit.
- CMS UI: the record modal lists versions (`vN`, action, actor, time) with View and Restore; View opens a diff panel (field, before, current) plus the snapshot's per-locale title/slug/body. `pnpm --dir tm-cms run typecheck` and `lint` pass.
- Public listing (Web): `GET /api/public/content` on the same publish policy as one record, no body in the projection, type/tag/category facets, `newest|oldest` order on `_id` with an exact cursor, requested-locale + `vi` fallback resolved in one query. Storefront page `/bai-viet` renders that listing with tag/category filters and load-more.
- Live evidence: revision list/detail/restore over HTTP (CMS `:4005`) produced `[update v2, create v1]`, restored v1 as v3 with content back to the original title/body, history showed `Reverted to version` and the modal rendered v1–v3; probe rows were removed. Web `:4006`: listing returned only published records (`tag=about` → 1, `tag=missing` → empty, `type=bogus`/bad cursor → `422`), `limit=2` pagination was exact across two pages (`posts/tin-c`, `posts/tin-b` then `posts/tin-a`, `pages/gioi-thieu`), `/bai-viet?tag=huong-dan` rendered one card plus the active-filter chip, and demo posts were removed afterwards.
- Remaining gaps (why these stay Partial): no category tree or hierarchy (CNT-01), no media attachments or the seven legacy editorial formats (CNT-02), no bulk actions, no tag/category landing pages beyond query filters (WEB-02), no public search (WEB-04), no durable revalidation events (WEB-09), and no retention/pruning policy for `content_revisions`.

### MED-01 / CNT-02 — P3.e media library record (2026-11-07)

- Target files: `tm-cms/server/models/media.ts` (collection `media`, unique `(siteId, storageKey)`, MIME/size guard, folder normalization, key builder), `tm-cms/server/utils/mediaStorage.ts` (root resolution, put/get/remove, checksum, path-traversal guard), `tm-cms/server/services/mediaService.ts` (`listMedia`, `createMedia`, `updateMedia`, `deleteMedia`, `findMediaByKey`), `tm-cms/server/api/v1/admin/media/{index.get,index.post,[id].patch,[id].delete}.ts`, the public `tm-cms/server/routes/media/[...key].get.ts`, migration `media-indexes` v5, `tm-cms/server/utils/modelRegistry.ts` (`Media`), and the audit model's new `media` resource type.
- Contracts: `@tm/flow-contracts/media-admin` — `AdminMediaSchema`, `AdminMediaListSchema`, `MediaUpdateSchema`, `MEDIA_MIME_TYPES`, `MEDIA_EXTENSIONS`, `MEDIA_MAX_BYTES`.
- Behaviour: bytes never enter MongoDB. An allowlisted upload (png/jpeg/webp/avif/gif/pdf, 5 MB) is stored under `<site>/<yyyy>/<uuid>.<ext>` with the extension derived from the MIME type; the row keeps title/alt/folder, kind, size and checksum. Storage keys are immutable, so the public route answers `immutable` forever. Deleting is refused while content still references the id. `media` on a content record is part of the draft, the revision snapshot, the restore path and `changedFields`.
- CMS UI: `/media` is a real library — multi-file upload with per-file inline errors, debounced search, kind/folder filters, `counts`/`folders` from `meta`, thumbnail grid with copy-URL/edit/delete, load more, plus empty and permission states. The content editor replaced the free-text attachments field with a searchable multi-select fed by the library and lists the attached files in the detail view.
- Tests: `pnpm test:media` — 11 live service checks (folder normalization, sanitized original name, allowlisted type only, size cap, checksum + on-disk bytes, list filters/counts/folders, metadata update with optimistic lock, delete refused while referenced then allowed, site scoping, one audit entry per mutation).
- Live evidence (CMS `:4005`): multipart `POST` → `201` with `key: sentuan/2026/<uuid>.png` and the folder normalized from `Landing / Hero` to `landing/hero`; `GET` of the returned URL answered `200 image/png` with `cache-control: public, max-age=31536000, immutable`, `x-content-type-options: nosniff` and `cross-origin-resource-policy: cross-origin`, and the downloaded bytes matched the upload; `PATCH` metadata → `revision 2`. In the browser the library rendered the thumbnail and the record, a second upload through the page's file input appeared without a reload, the edit dialog saved a new title/alt (`Revision 2`), and the content editor's picker listed both files and saved `media: ["<id>"]` on `pages/dich-vu`. Deleting the attached file from the library returned `422 MEDIA_IN_USE` with `This file is used by content; detach it from that content first` kept inside the dialog and the file still listed. Afterwards the attachment was detached, both files deleted (bytes removed from `.data/media`), and the media rows plus the media/content audit and revision rows from the verification were removed.
- Remaining gaps (why MED-01 stays Partial): no provider adapter (local disk only), no image transforms/thumbnails (the grid loads the original), no width/height capture, no folder tree (folders are typed names), no idempotency for multipart uploads, no inline upload from the rich-text editor, and the storefront does not render media yet — the public projection still has no media field.
- Gates: `pnpm test:media` 11, `pnpm test:content` 26, `tm-cms` `typecheck` and `lint` pass, `build:cms` succeeds.

### CNT-01 / OPS-01 — P3.f bulk content actions record (2026-11-07)

- Target files: `tm-cms/server/services/contentBulk.ts` (`runBulkContentCommand`, `BULK_MAX_ITEMS = 50`), `tm-cms/server/api/v1/admin/content/bulk.post.ts`, `tm-shared/packages/flow-contracts/src/content-admin.ts` (`ADMIN_CONTENT_BULK_ACTIONS`, `AdminContentBulkItemSchema`, `AdminContentBulkRequestSchema`, `AdminContentBulkRowSchema`, `AdminContentBulkResultSchema`), `tm-shared/nuxt-layers/cms-base/app/composables/useContentAdmin.ts` (`bulk()`), `tm-shared/nuxt-layers/cms-base/app/pages/content.vue` (select-all bar, per-row checkbox, action dialog with a per-row result list), `tm-cms/i18n/locales/{vi,en}.json` (`admin.content.bulk.*`).
- Behaviour: one request carries 1–50 `{ id, revision }` items and one of `publish | archive | delete`. The service loops the single-record command, so every row keeps its own optimistic lock, audit entry, revision snapshot and `publishedAt`/`deletedAt` semantics. A stale revision, an invalid transition, a foreign or unknown id and a duplicated id only fail their own row — nothing is rolled back, and the request still answers `200` with `{ action, results[], succeeded, failed }`. `schedule` is excluded because each record needs its own `publishAt`. No `Idempotency-Key` is needed: a retry re-sends the same revisions, already-applied rows answer `CONTENT_TRANSITION_INVALID` and nothing new is written.
- Tests: `pnpm test:bulk` — 9 live checks in a disposable `tm_test_bulk_service` database: both rows applied and reported, a stale revision failing alone while its neighbour still moves, an invalid transition as a row error, unknown/foreign id as a per-row `CONTENT_NOT_FOUND`, a duplicated id reported once as `CONTENT_BULK_DUPLICATE`, one audit entry + one snapshot per row (`[delete, publish]`, revisions `[3, 2, 1]`), a retry that cannot re-apply, the revision of a bulk-edited record still driving later writes, and the 1–50/action contract.
- Live HTTP (CMS `:4005`, `sentuan`): `POST /bulk` `publish` over two drafts → `200`, `succeeded: 2`, both rows `published` at `revision 2`; the same items at their old revisions → `200`, `failed: 2`, every row `CONTENT_REVISION_CONFLICT`; `{"action":"schedule","items":[]}` → `422 CONTENT_BULK_INVALID` listing both schema details.
- Browser (`/content`, 1440×900): the select-all bar reported `0 selected` and kept the three actions disabled until rows were checked; two selected records archived through the dialog → `Processed 2/2 records`, both rows `Archived`, the selection cleared and the counts refreshed; a run over an already-archived record → `Processed 0/1 records` with that row's localized `That action is not allowed in the current status`.
- Note: the select-all run also covered the two demo records (`pages/gioi-thieu`, `pages/dich-vu`), which were restored by hand afterwards (status/revision/`deletedAt` back to `published` v6 and `draft` v9, with the verification's audit and revision rows removed). The post's `publishedAt` had been cleared by the soft delete, so it carries the restore time instead of its original publish time.
- Gates: `pnpm test:bulk` 9 checks, `tm-cms` `typecheck` and `lint` pass, `build:cms` succeeds (34.4s).
- Remaining gaps (why CNT-01/OPS-01 stay Partial): bulk is limited to publish/archive/delete over the loaded page (no server-side "all matching the filter"), no bulk schedule, no bulk edit of shared fields, no undo, no progress for batches beyond the 50-row cap, no media batch move/delete, and no import/export or background job runner yet.

### WEB-02 — discovery landing pages and bounded public search record (2026-11-07)

- Target files (Web): `tm-web/server/models/contentCategoryPublic.ts` (read-only `categories` view, locale-slug filter, `toPublicContentCategory`), `tm-web/server/utils/publicCategories.ts` (`fetchPublicContentCategory`, `fetchPublicContentCategories`, `fetchPublicContentTags`), `tm-web/server/api/public/content/categories/index.get.ts`, `.../categories/[slug].get.ts`, `tm-web/server/models/contentPublic.ts` (`escapePublicSearchTerm`, `q` in `buildPublishedListFilter`), `tm-web/server/api/public/content/index.get.ts` (`q` ≤80), `tm-web/server/routes/sitemap.xml.ts` (tag/category URLs).
- Target files (storefront layer): `tm-shared/nuxt-layers/web-base/app/components/WebContentList.vue` (shared card grid, states, load-more), `.../pages/tag/[tag].vue`, `.../pages/danh-muc/[slug].vue`, `.../pages/bai-viet/index.vue` (search box + `q`), `.../pages/[...slug].vue` (tag chips link to the tag page), `tm-web/i18n/locales/{vi,en}.json` (`post.search*`, `tag.*`, `categoryContent.*`).
- Contracts: `@tm/flow-contracts/content-public` — `PublicContentCategorySchema`, `PublicContentCategoryListSchema`, `PUBLIC_CONTENT_SEARCH_MAX = 80`.
- Behaviour: search is literal (regex metacharacters escaped) and case-insensitive over the localized title/slug/excerpt/keywords plus the key, keeps the published/window/locale policy and combines with `tag`/`category`/`type`. A longer term is `422`, an empty one is ignored. Category resolution maps the URL slug to the immutable key and falls back to `vi`; an unknown slug is `404`, while a category without published content shows the empty state. Tags have no registry, so the tag page lists published records by that string and the sitemap derives tags from published rows.
- Tests: `pnpm test:web-content` — 25 contract checks (category DTO, search cap) plus 20 live reader checks in a disposable `tm_test_public_content` database: search on title/slug/excerpt case-insensitively, regex metacharacters treated literally (`.*` → no rows, `[` → no rows), search combined with a tag while drafts and other locales stay hidden, a keyword matching only inside the searched locale, category resolution by `vi`/`en` slug, `vi` fallback and unknown slug.
- Live HTTP (Web `:4006`, `sentuan`): `categories` → `200 {data: []}`; unknown category slug → `404`; `?q=gioi` → `pages/gioi-thieu`; `?q=.*` → 0 rows; an 81-character `q` → `422`; `/tag/about` → `200` with `<h1>#about</h1>`; `/danh-muc/khong-co` → `404`; sitemap listed `/tag/about` and `/tag/silver`. With a probe category `trang-suc` (vi `Trang sức`, en `Jewelry`) and a probe published post `posts/tin-thu`: `categories` returned the key/name pair, `/api/public/content?category=trang-suc` and `?q=tin` both returned the post, `/danh-muc/trang-suc` rendered `Jewelry` + 1 card, `/danh-muc/jewelry` resolved the same category with its own canonical, `/tag/about` rendered 2 cards, submitting the search box navigated to `/bai-viet?q=bản` and matched the post's excerpt, and the tag chip on `/posts/tin-thu` navigated to `/tag/about`. Browser console showed no errors.
- Cleanup: the probe post and category were deleted; the database returned to `content 2 / categories 0 / content_revisions 0 / audit_logs 0 / media 0`, the category page 404s again and the sitemap lists only the demo content and its tags.
- Gates: `pnpm test:web-content` (25 + 19), `pnpm test:layers`, `tm-web` `typecheck` and `lint` pass, `build:web` succeeds (47.3s; an earlier attempt was interrupted by a runner restart and re-run, not trusted from the partial output).
- Remaining gaps (why WEB-02 stays Partial): explicit redirects (301/302) and the 410 gone page landed on 2026-11-10/11, see the P3.g and WEB-08.t records, catalog item/category routes are still legacy, and media is still absent from the public projection — wiring it needs a decision on the public media origin, because the CMS stores bytes locally while the Web is a separate deployment. The category index and the display-name badge were closed on 2026-11-09 (see the record below).

### WEB-02 — content category index and display-name badge record (2026-11-09)

- Target files: `tm-shared/nuxt-layers/web-base/app/composables/usePublicContentCategories.ts` (new — one locale-scoped read of `/api/public/content/categories` shared by every page, indexed by key, failing read degrades to an empty map), `.../app/pages/danh-muc/index.vue` (new index page), `.../app/pages/bai-viet/index.vue` (badge resolves the key), `.../app/composables/usePublicSite.ts` (fallback navigation), `tm-web/server/routes/sitemap.xml.ts` (index URL), `tm-web/i18n/locales/{vi,en}.json` (`nav.posts`, `nav.postCategories`, `categoryContent.index*`/`empty*`/`browseAll`/`allPosts`). No model, contract or API change: the category list endpoint already existed and still returns the same DTO.
- Behaviour: a content record stores the category `key`, so the listing now maps it through the category row and shows the visitor-facing name, linking the badge to `/danh-muc/<slug>`; an unknown key still prints exactly as stored and stays unlinked rather than turning into a dead link. The new index page groups rows by parent key so a child sits under its parent, promotes a child whose parent is not published in this locale instead of dropping it, and renders an empty state (with a link back to the listing) when a site has no categories. The index is only added to the sitemap while at least one category exists, because an empty index is a thin page. Nothing links to a category that the API did not return.
- Gates: `tm-web` `typecheck` and `lint` pass; `pnpm test:web-content` 25 contract + 19 live reader checks pass.
- Browser (Web `:4006`, probe parent `probe-noi-that` + child `probe-noi-that-sub` in `categories`, probe published post in the child): `/danh-muc` rendered one group `Probe Nội thất` with `Probe Phòng khách` beneath it, each linking to its own slug, canonical `/danh-muc`; `/bai-viet?category=probe-noi-that-sub` showed the badge `Probe Phòng khách` linked to `/danh-muc/probe-phong-khach` instead of the key, plus the "Xem tất cả danh mục bài viết" link; `/danh-muc/probe-phong-khach` rendered the child name and the probe post; the header gained `Bài viết` with a `Danh mục bài viết` child and the footer listed both; switching to English re-read the same rows as `Probe Furniture` / `Probe Living room` with `Posts` / `Post categories` labels; `?category=khong-ton-tai` printed the raw key, unlinked, with the empty listing. The sitemap listed `/danh-muc` and both slugs while the categories existed.
- Cleanup: the probe post was deleted through the admin event (`200`), the two probe categories removed (`DELETE` `200` twice, audit rows for `resourceType: category` cleared), and the database returned to `contents 8 (7 legacy pages + 1 legacy post) / categories 0 / content_revisions 0 / audit_logs 0 / idempotency_keys 0`, with the 6 legacy `content_categories` rows untouched. `/danh-muc` now renders its empty state, the category list API returns `[]` and the sitemap no longer contains a `danh-muc` URL.
- Write path: the category tree does have a CMS surface (P3.e — `/api/v1/admin/categories`, `content.write`, collection `categories`), and a second pass created the parent and child through that API instead of touching Mongo: both `201`, the public list and `/danh-muc` showed the new rows within a second, the sitemap listed both slugs, and `DELETE ?revision=1` (child, then parent) returned `200`. The first pass had inserted its probe rows directly, which is why this record says so; nothing in the slice depends on that shortcut. The linked legacy post still stores a legacy `content_categories` `_id` rather than a category key, so its badge keeps printing that value until the import decides the mapping (Q2/Q5).

### WEB-04 — public search page record (2026-11-08)

- Target files (storefront): `tm-shared/nuxt-layers/web-base/app/pages/tim-kiem.vue` (bounded query, two result groups, hint/empty/partial-catalog states), `.../components/WebSearchHighlight.vue` (escape-then-`<mark>` highlighter), `.../layouts/default.vue` (nav entry), `tm-web/i18n/locales/{vi,en}.json` (`search.*`, `contentType.*`, `nav.search`).
- Behaviour: no new server route. The page sends the same trimmed, 80-character-capped `q` to `/api/public/content?q=` and `/api/public/products?search=` (through `useCatalogList`), so content keeps its published/window/locale policy and the catalog keeps `status: published + isActive`; each group is capped at 8 rows with a "view all" link into `/bai-viet?q=` or `/products?search=` instead of a merged cursor that would need a cross-collection tie-breaker. Result pages are `noindex, follow`.
- Highlighting: the record's title/excerpt is HTML-escaped first and only `<mark>` is added, with the term escaped before it becomes a pattern — a record title or a query containing `<`/`*` cannot inject markup or match as a wildcard. Product cards reuse `CatalogProductCard` and are not highlighted.
- Evidence (Web `:4006`, `sentuan`): `/tim-kiem` without `q` rendered the hint and no requests; `?q=Giới` rendered 1 content row (`/pages/gioi-thieu`) with `<mark>Giới</mark>` inside the title; `?q=khongcoketqua` rendered the empty state; the input carries `maxlength="80"` and the page carries `robots: noindex, follow`. Product search against the real catalog: `?q=hn-bo` rendered `Products 6` with the six `/products/bo-hn-bo*` cards and `/products?search=hn-bo` as the view-all link; `?q=ban` returned 3; a seeded probe product appeared as one card and was removed again afterwards.
- Catalog data check on the same run: the `items` collection holds 241 records (229 published), and all 241 carry the legacy denormalized `searchString` the catalog filter matches — so product search works on the imported data, but a future catalog write path must keep maintaining that field.
- Cleanup: probe product deleted (`removedItems: 1`); the database returned to `content 2 / items 241 / categories 0 / content_revisions 0 / audit_logs 0`.
- Gates: `tm-web` `typecheck` and `lint` pass, `pnpm test:layers` passes (the new i18n messages are ICU-safe), `build:web` succeeds (47.1s).
- Remaining gaps (why WEB-04 stays Partial): no pagination inside a result group (top 8 plus view-all only), no typo tolerance/synonyms/normalization beyond what each endpoint already does, highlighting covers content title/excerpt but not product names, category/tag discovery is served by their own landing pages rather than search facets, no zero-result suggestions, and no search analytics or query logging.

### WEB-08 — robots.txt and crawl policy record (2026-11-08)

- Target files: `tm-shared/packages/flow-utils/src/seo.ts` (`buildRobotsTxt`), `tm-web/server/routes/robots.txt.ts` (route, cache headers, `config.public.indexable`), `scripts/build/test-web-content.ts` (both branches).
- Behaviour: an indexable deployment allows the storefront and disallows only `/api/` and `/tim-kiem` (search URLs depend on a visitor query and are marked `noindex, follow`), advertising `<base>/sitemap.xml` on the canonical origin; a deployment with `TM_SITE_INDEXABLE=false` answers `User-agent: *` + `Disallow: /`, matching the page-level robots meta. The route reuses `getSiteBaseUrl`, so a configured `TM_SITE_URL` wins over the forwarded host.
- Evidence (Web `:4006`): `GET /robots.txt` → `200 text/plain`, body `User-agent: * / Allow: / / Disallow: /api/ / Disallow: /tim-kiem / Sitemap: http://localhost:4006/sitemap.xml`, `Cache-Control: public, max-age=300, s-maxage=600, stale-while-revalidate=3600`; `pnpm test:web-content` covers both the public and the preview branch (27 contract checks). `sitemap.xml` already lists content, tag and category URLs.
- Deliberate gap: hreflang is not emitted. The storefront serves one locale-free URL per record (`?locale=` switches the language, no `/vi` `/en` prefixes), so alternates would point at the same URL; adding them would require a locale-prefixed URL policy first.
- Gates: `pnpm test:web-content` (27 + 19), `pnpm test:layers`, root `pnpm typecheck` and `pnpm lint` (all workspaces), `build:web` 46.5s.
- Remaining gaps (why WEB-08 stays Partial): no locale-prefixed URLs/hreflang, no per-record OG image generation, no structured data beyond WebPage/Article/Product/CollectionPage and their breadcrumb and list companions, no preview-deployment `noindex` header for non-HTML responses, and no crawl report/robots tests against a real deployment.

### WEB-05 — cart record (2026-11-08)

- Files: `tm-shared/packages/flow-contracts/src/public-cart.ts` (schemas, caps, pure `buildPublicCart`), `tm-web/server/utils/cartService.ts` (published-catalog lookup + mapping), `tm-web/server/api/public/cart/validate.post.ts`, `tm-shared/nuxt-layers/web-base/app/composables/useCart.ts`, `.../pages/gio-hang.vue`, purchase panel in `.../pages/products/[slug].vue`, cart badge in the layout, `cart.*` i18n, `scripts/build/test-web-cart.ts`.
- Policy: storage holds ids/SKUs/quantities and the last acknowledged price only; every price, line total, subtotal and availability flag is recomputed server-side under the same `published + isActive + not deleted` filter as the catalog. A variant product without a SKU is unquotable (`variant_unavailable`) rather than silently priced from the base product. Unavailable lines stay visible and removable but never count toward the subtotal. Caps: 20 lines, quantity 1–99, `422 CART_INVALID` beyond that. The catalog detail carries `maxQuantity` for a simple product and for each variant when stock is managed; a larger line is clamped to that number, reported as `quantity_reduced`, and the clamped quantity is stored back by the client.
- Evidence (contract): `pnpm test:web-cart` → 16 checks (schema caps, simple/variant pricing, missing/unknown SKU, unknown item, out-of-stock, simple and variant stock caps, moved price, decimal rounding, ordering/aggregation, response schema, line keys).
- Evidence (HTTP, `:4006` against probe records in `sen-tuan`): simple product `expectedPrice` match → `unitPrice 150000`, `lineTotal 300000`, `itemCount 2`; variant `CP-M` → `120000` available, no-SKU line → `variant_unavailable`, `CP-L` (stock 0) → `out_of_stock` with price preserved, unknown id → `item_unavailable` with `unitPrice 0`; stale `expectedPrice 99000` → `price_changed` + current `150000`; quantity `100` and 21 lines both `422 CART_INVALID`; response carried `Cache-Control: no-store`.
- Evidence (browser, `:4006`): product page added 2 units → badge `2`, `localStorage` `[{itemId, quantity: 2, expectedPrice: 150000}]`; `/gio-hang` showed `₫150,000` × 2 = `₫300,000`; quantity `3` re-quoted to `₫450,000`; variant product preselected the in-stock `CP-M` (out-of-stock `CP-L` disabled) and adding it produced two rows, `₫570,000` subtotal; raising the catalog price re-rendered the row at `₫160,000` with "The price was updated to the current store price." once, and the reload afterwards showed no notice; deleting the record produced "Product no longer sold" + `item_unavailable` excluded from the subtotal; remove dropped the row and the badge, and "Clear cart" restored the empty state with `localStorage` `[]`. Probe records were deleted afterwards (`items` back to 241) and the cart storage cleared.
- Gates: `test:web-cart`, `test:web-content` (27 + 19), `test:content`, `test:categories`, `test:media` (11), `test:bulk` (9), `test:iam`, `test:db`, `test:layers`, root `typecheck`, root `lint`, `build:web` 49.6s.
- Remaining gaps (why WEB-05 stays Partial): no stock reservation, no cross-device cart (needs an account/session), no coupon or shipping estimate, no server-side persistence of abandoned carts, and no checkout handoff (WEB-06).

### MED-01 — bulk media operations record (2026-11-08)

- Files: `AdminMediaBulk*Schema` in `@tm/flow-contracts/media-admin`, `runBulkMediaCommand` in `tm-cms/server/services/mediaService.ts`, `tm-cms/server/api/v1/admin/media/bulk.post.ts`, `bulk()` in `useMediaAdmin`, selection bar + move/delete dialogs in `cms-base/app/pages/media.vue`, `admin.media.bulk.*` i18n, extra checks in `scripts/build/test-media-service.ts`.
- Policy: no new storage or locking rules — every row calls `updateMedia`/`deleteMedia`, so folder normalization, `revision` optimistic lock, `MEDIA_IN_USE` refusal and the audit entry stay in one place. Caps: 1–50 files per request; duplicate ids are reported as `MEDIA_BULK_DUPLICATE` instead of being applied twice; capability is `media.upload` for `move` and `media.delete` for `delete`.
- Evidence (service, disposable DB + temp media dir): `pnpm test:media` → 12 checks, including a bulk case that moves two files (`landing/banner`, revisions `1 → 2`), reports `MEDIA_UPDATE_UNCHANGED` + `MEDIA_BULK_DUPLICATE` for a repeated id, `MEDIA_REVISION_CONFLICT` for a stale revision, throws `MEDIA_FOLDER_INVALID` for an over-long folder, and deletes a free file while refusing the one attached to content (`MEDIA_IN_USE`).
- Evidence (HTTP, CMS `:4005` with mock auth against `sen-tuan`, temporary probe files): three uploads → `201`; `POST /bulk move` with `Landing / Banner` → `200`, `folder: landing/banner`, both rows `ok`, `revision: 2`; duplicate + stale revision → `0/2` with `MEDIA_REVISION_CONFLICT` / `MEDIA_BULK_DUPLICATE`; empty items, unknown action and 51 items → `422`; delete of an attached + a free file → `1/1` with `MEDIA_IN_USE`, the attached file kept.
- Evidence (browser, `:4005`): the media page rendered per-card checkboxes; selecting two files showed "2 files selected" with *Move to folder* / *Delete selected*; the move dialog pre-filled the shared folder, and `Landing / Featured` moved both files (list re-rendered at `landing/featured`, revision 3, selection cleared); the delete dialog reported one failure inline ("bulk-one.png — This file is attached to 1 content record(s); detach it first") while the other file disappeared and only the failed row stayed selected.
- Probe data was removed afterwards: `content 2 / media 0 / content_revisions 0 / audit_logs 0 / items 241`, stored media files deleted, temp upload directory removed, and the CMS dev server stopped.
- Gates: `test:media` (12), root `typecheck`, root `lint`.
- Verification gap: modal *dismissal* could not be observed in the automated preview — every dialog (including the untouched `/categories` page) keeps its node mounted with `data-state="closed"` because the exit animation never advances in this tab. That is an environment/tooling limit, not a difference introduced by this slice, but it means "dialog closes after the run" is unverified.
- Remaining gaps: bulk is limited to the selected page of results (no "all matching the filter"), no undo, no bulk title/alt edit, no drag-and-drop between folders, and no media in the public content projection (blocked on Q13).

### CNT-01 / P3.f (rest) — tag management record (2026-11-10)

- Files: `tm-shared/packages/flow-contracts/src/tag-admin.ts` (new; `AdminTag`, `AdminTagRenameRequestSchema`, `AdminTagRenameResult`, `ADMIN_TAG_MAX_LENGTH`) plus the package's subpath export and root re-export, `tm-cms/server/services/tagService.ts` (new; `listTags`, `renameTag`, `replaceTag`, `TagServiceError`), `tm-cms/server/api/v1/admin/tags/{index.get,rename.post}.ts` (new), `tm-cms/server/utils/contentApi.ts` (shared schema re-export, `TagServiceError` translation), `cms-base/app/composables/useTagAdmin.ts` (new), `cms-base/app/pages/tags.vue` (new), `cms-base/app/layouts/default.vue` (nav item), `tm-cms/i18n/locales/{vi,en}.json` (`nav.tags`, `admin.tags.*`), `scripts/build/test-tag-service.ts` (new), root `package.json` (`test:tags`).
- Shape: tags have no collection of their own — they are strings on the content records — so the admin surface is a projection plus one command. `GET /api/v1/admin/tags` aggregates the site's records (`unwind` → `group` → count desc, name asc, capped at 500) and includes deleted records on purpose, so a tag that survives only on deleted content can still be cleaned up. `POST /api/v1/admin/tags/rename` takes `{ from, to }` (or `to: null` to detach everything), is idempotent through the shared key, and requires `content.write`; the read requires `content.read`.
- Command semantics: every record that carries the tag is rewritten under its own `revision` guard, so an editor's concurrent save is reported as `skipped: n` instead of being clobbered. The replacement keeps the tag's position in the list, a merge onto an existing tag drops the duplicate (`['a','typo','b']` + `typo → b` becomes `['a','b']`), and detaching never removes the record. Each rewritten record gets an `update` audit entry with `changedFields: ["tags"]` and a revision snapshot, so the existing content history explains the change and a restore can put the tag back.
- Route choice: `/api/v1/admin/tags`, not `/api/v1/admin/content/tags`, because the content detail route is a single dynamic segment and a static child would shadow a record id called `tags`.
- Evidence (service, disposable DB): `pnpm test:tags` → 9 checks: position-preserving replacement and merge de-duplication; site-scoped ordered counts; rename of two records bumping `revision` to 2 while another site keeps its copy; merge leaving one tag; detach from three records leaving the records; `404 TAG_NOT_FOUND`, `422 TAG_RENAME_SAME`, `422 TAG_REQUIRED`, `422 TAG_TOO_LONG`; whitespace-normalized `from` with a retry that finds nothing; audit row plus snapshot per record; a stale row reported as `{ updated: 0, skipped: 1 }` with the competing save intact.
- Evidence (HTTP, CMS `:4005`, mock auth, `sen-tuan`): two probe pages created through the content API with overlapping tags → the tag list showed both at count 2 above the real tags; rename → `{ updated: 2, skipped: 0 }` and the new name at 2; merge onto the other tag → `{ updated: 2 }`, one tag left at 2; detach → `{ updated: 2, to: null }`, probe tags gone; blank `from` → `422`, foreign `Origin` → `403`, anonymous → `401`.
- Evidence (browser, `:4005/tags`): list with counts and a `6 thẻ · 6 lượt gắn` summary; the rename dialog pre-filled the name, typing an existing tag showed the merge warning (`Thẻ "silver" đã tồn tại (1 nội dung)`), saving renamed and refreshed the list with a toast; the detach dialog reported `Đã gỡ thẻ khỏi 1 nội dung` and the tag disappeared.
- Cleanup: probe pages, their revisions and audit rows deleted; `content 3 / categories 0 / audit_logs 0 / content_revisions 0 / idempotency_keys 0`.
- Remaining gaps: sequential `O(records with the tag)` command that reports conflicts instead of retrying them; list capped at 500 with no pagination; no tag-level audit resource (the trail is per record), no attribute editor and no global keyword collection (CNT-02; keywords themselves have a per-locale editor since 2026-11-10), and the storefront still reads tags from the published projection only.

### CNT-01 / P3.e (rest) — drag-and-drop category ordering record (2026-11-10)

- Files: `tm-shared/packages/flow-contracts/src/category-admin.ts` (`AdminCategoryReorderRequestSchema`), `tm-cms/server/services/categoryService.ts` (`reorderCategory`, `writeCategoryPosition`), `tm-cms/server/api/v1/admin/categories/reorder.post.ts` (new), `cms-base/app/composables/useCategoryAdmin.ts` (`reorder`), `cms-base/app/pages/categories.vue` (draggable rows, before/inside/after drop zones, root zone, hint), `tm-cms/i18n/locales/{vi,en}.json` (`admin.categories.{dragHint,rootDrop,moved}`), `scripts/build/test-category-reorder.ts` (new), root `package.json` (`test:category-order`).
- Shape: one command, `{ key, parent, index }` under `content.write` and idempotency (`category.reorder:<key>`). It reuses `assertParentAllowed`, so cycles and the five-level depth limit are refused exactly as in the dialog, and it answers with the flat list plus the ordered tree so the page renders server truth. The editor sends one move per drop: top third of a row = before, bottom third = after, middle = inside, plus a dashed top-level zone while dragging; rows inside the dragged subtree refuse the pointer.
- Ordering policy: sibling `order` values are spaced by ten and a drop takes the midpoint between its neighbours, so a normal move writes one row. When two neighbours are adjacent, or a bucket runs out of `CATEGORY_MAX_ORDER` room, the whole bucket is renumbered once with a spacing derived from the maximum; only rows whose value changed are written. A drop back into the current position is detected by comparing the position in the bucket (not the computed midpoint) and writes nothing.
- Evidence (service, disposable DB): `pnpm test:category-order` → 8 checks: reparent writes one row and leaves siblings at their revision; sibling drop honours `index`; midpoint drop keeps orders unique; squeezed bucket renumbers once with unique in-range orders and room between rows; no-op drop keeps the revision; `422 CATEGORY_CYCLE` into a descendant and onto itself; `422 CATEGORY_PARENT_NOT_FOUND` and `404 CATEGORY_NOT_FOUND` including another site; one audit row per changed row with `parent`/`order` in `changedFields`.
- Evidence (HTTP, `:4005`): two probe roots → reorder `probe-order-b` into `probe-order-a` at index 0 → `200`, `parent: probe-order-a`, `order: 10`, `revision: 2`; back to the root at index 0 → `order: 890`; cycle attempt → `422`.
- Evidence (browser, `:4005/categories`): native `DragEvent`s from `probe-order-b` onto the middle of `probe-order-a` highlighted the target, toasted `Đã cập nhật vị trí danh mục` and rendered `probe-order-b` as a child (`order: 10`, `revision: 4`); the root zone highlighted and moved it back to the top level (`order: 910`, `revision: 5`), confirmed by the list API. Probe categories and audit rows removed (`categories 0 / audit_logs 0 / idempotency_keys 0 / content 3`).
- Bumped rows keep the same optimistic lock as the dialog, so a stale tree tab gets `409 CATEGORY_REVISION_CONFLICT` and a reload rather than overwriting a concurrent edit.
- Remaining gaps: no pointer-based touch path on this page (menus have one), so a phone still edits `order` in the dialog; no multi-select or bulk move; the renumber path writes every row in the bucket; the storefront reads category order from the existing public categories endpoint, unchanged.

### CNT-01 (rest) — tag filter on the content list (landed 2026-11-10)

- Target files: `tm-cms/server/services/contentService.ts` (`ContentListParams.tag`, exact match, 60-character cap), `tm-cms/server/utils/contentApi.ts` (`ContentListQuerySchema.tag`: trimmed, 1–60 characters), `tm-shared/nuxt-layers/cms-base/app/composables/useContentAdmin.ts` (`ContentListQuery.tag`), `tm-shared/nuxt-layers/cms-base/app/pages/content.vue` (tag select fed by `useTagAdmin`, `all` sentinel, reload on change), `tm-cms/i18n/locales/{vi,en}.json` (`admin.content.tag.{all,placeholder}`), `scripts/build/test-content-service.ts` (one new check).
- Behaviour: `GET /api/v1/admin/content?tag=…` adds `tags: "<tag>"` to the Mongo filter, so it composes with `status`, `type`, `q` and cursor pagination. The value is trimmed and capped server-side; the UI sends the exact name from the tag list, so there is no `$regex` and no index cost beyond the existing multikey array. `meta.counts` stays whole-site like every other filter, so the editor can see the filtered set against the site total.
- Editor: the list toolbar gains a fourth select fed by `GET /api/v1/admin/tags`, labelled `name (count)`, defaulting to "Tất cả thẻ". The options are fetched once on mount; a failed option fetch leaves the select empty instead of breaking the list, and a rename done on `/tags` appears on the next visit because nothing is cached.
- Reka detail: `SelectItem` rejects an empty-string value (the page renders a 500 with "A <SelectItem /> must have a value prop that is not an empty string"), so the default option uses an `all` sentinel that is translated to "no tag parameter" before the request.
- Evidence (service, disposable DB): `pnpm test:content` → 28 checks (one new): the filter returns exactly the tagged record, two records sharing a tag both come back, a padded value still matches, it composes with `status` (draft → 2 rows, published → 0), an unknown tag returns 0 rows while `counts` still equal the unfiltered counts, and another site's identically tagged record never leaks.
- Evidence (HTTP, `:4005`): `tag=dịch vụ` → 1 (`pages/dich-vu`), `tag=about` → 1 (`pages/gioi-thieu`), `tag=probe` → 1 (`probe-category-post`), `tag=khong-co` → 0, `tag=about&status=published` → 1, a blank tag → `422`, a 61-character tag → `422`, anonymous → `401`; every response kept `meta.counts.all = 3`.
- Evidence (browser, `:4005/content`): the toolbar select listed `Tất cả thẻ / about (1) / dịch vụ (1) / probe (1) / silver (1) / tư vấn (1)`; choosing `about (1)` issued `GET /api/v1/admin/content?status=all&tag=about&limit=20 → 200` and left exactly `pages/gioi-thieu` in the list, with no console errors.
- Limits: one tag at a time (no "any of these tags"), no negation, and the option list refreshes only on mount; the list itself is still page-limited to 50 rows per request like every other filter.

### P4.a / CAT-01 — catalog CRUD on CMS-owned collections (landed 2026-11-10)

- Decision: the CMS writes **new** collections (`catalog_items`, `catalog_categories`) and the storefront reader was re-pointed at them; the legacy `items`/`item_categories` are left untouched for rollback. This is the direction `docs/v2/04` describes and avoids `docs/v2/01`'s warning about turning the legacy reader into a write schema. Recorded as Q23 in [questions](../tasks/questions.md).
- Models: `tm-cms/server/models/catalog.ts` — localized title/slug/short description/description, `basePrice`/`salePrice`, SKU/uom/type/stock/manageStock, category ids, tags, attributes, variants, SEO, `searchString`, `sales`/`views` counters (kept so the `popular` sort still works), status (`draft|published|archived|deleted`), `isActive`/`isDeleted`, revision, timestamps. Unique indexes on (siteId, key) and (siteId, slug.vi); soft delete keeps the key, exactly like content.
- Service: `tm-cms/server/services/catalogService.ts` — list with status/type/tag/search/category (subtree), counts, cursor paging; create/update with revision conflict, immutable key, category resolution, `searchString` rebuild; publish/archive/restore/delete transitions; category create/update with subtree path + ancestors recomputation (cycle guard), delete refused while children or items remain; per-site tag aggregation.
- API: `/api/v1/admin/catalog/items` (+ `[id]`, `[id]/events`), `/api/v1/admin/catalog/categories` (+ `[id]`), `/api/v1/admin/catalog/tags`; same capability (`content.read`/`content.write`), CSRF and idempotency wrappers as content.
- Editor: `cms-base/app/pages/catalog.vue` (replaces the placeholder) with filters, counts, row actions, an item editor modal (both locales, prices, SKU, stock, categories, tags, images, SEO) and a flat category manager (parent select, rename, description, image URL, SEO title/description, guarded delete). i18n `admin.catalog.*` in both locales.
- Migration: `pnpm migrate:catalog --site sen-tuan [--apply]` (`scripts/ops/migrate-catalog.ts`), dry-run by default, idempotent by `legacyId`, preserves `sales`/`views`, maps category ids/ancestors, and suffixes a colliding key/slug so no legacy row is lost (one legacy pair shares `man-khung-ngang`). Legacy tags are `{ title, slug }` documents, so they go through the same `tagNames` rule the content importer uses (display name, deduped, 50 × 60 caps); until 2026-11-18 the catalog importer passed them through `text()` and silently dropped every one. Re-applying on `sen-tuan` restored tags on 230 of 241 rows (164 distinct values) and their `searchString`. Where tags are consumed: the CMS catalog list's `tag` filter and `GET /api/v1/admin/catalog/tags` (both empty before the fix), and public product search, which matches `searchString` — `?q=interior` now finds the 6 tagged items. There is deliberately no public tag facet on products; the storefront's tag pages belong to content.
- Storefront: `tm-web/server/models/catalog.ts` reads `catalog_items`/`catalog_categories`; the subtree filter uses the stored `ancestors` array. Product/category/search/sitemap/cart paths needed no other change because the public mapper's field names are preserved.
- Evidence (service, disposable DB): `pnpm test:catalog` → 18 checks: normalization, parent path/ancestors, search string, duplicate key → `409`, invalid price → `422`, unknown category → `422`, publish visibility, stale revision → `409` (record unchanged), immutable key, counter preservation, composed filters + subtree, cross-site isolation, tag aggregation, delete guards, move rewriting the path, delete/restore transitions.
- Evidence (migration, `sen-tuan`): dry run reported 67 categories / 241 items; `--apply` wrote 67 categories and **241 items (229 published)**, matching the legacy counts exactly, and re-running wrote the same rows (no duplicates, no lost record after the collision fix). Legacy collections still hold their original documents.
- Evidence (HTTP CMS `:4005`): `GET /api/v1/admin/catalog/items` → 241 rows, `counts {all:241, draft:3, published:229, deleted:9}`; categories → 67 with `itemCount`; tags → empty (legacy items carry no tags); anonymous → `401`; foreign-Origin mutation → `403`. A disposable probe item was created, published, fetched from the storefront, then deleted and purged from `catalog_items`/`audit_logs` (DB back to 241 items, audit 0).
- Evidence (storefront `:4006`): `/products/ruot-goi-thuong` → 200 with the legacy title and price 120.000, `/categories/chan-ga-goi-dem` → 200 listing 8 products, `?search=goi` returns the migrated records (searchString rebuilt), `/sitemap.xml` lists **229** product URLs (equal to the published count), `/api/public/cart/validate` re-quotes a migrated item.
- Evidence (browser `:4005/catalog`): page renders 241 records with status/type/tag filters and `Tổng: 241 / Đã xuất bản: 229`; the edit modal opens with key locked and every field populated (title/slug/prices/SKU/uom/stock/3 categories); no console errors.
- Limits: media fields are URL strings, not media-library picks (blocked by Q19); the catalog category manager is a flat list with a parent select, without drag ordering.
- Category image and SEO (2026-11-16): the category draft now carries `description`, `thumbnail` and `seo.{title,description}`; create/update persist them, the admin projection returns them, and the public category carries `image` plus `seo` for the category page's title/description. `test:catalog` creates a category with all four fields and asserts they round-trip. The storefront falls back to the category name/description when a SEO value is empty. The category panel now edits both locales for title, slug, description and SEO. `buildCatalogCategoryDraft` writes `vi` and `en` explicitly, keeps any other locale the record carries, and removes an English value when the editor clears it. `test:catalog` covers update, clear and create; in the browser a probe category was saved with all five English fields changed, then saved again with them blank: the API kept the Vietnamese values and dropped the English ones, and the probe category plus its four audit rows were removed. The form also edits `slug.vi`; an empty slug falls back to the Vietnamese title, while an existing slug is kept when only the title changes, and `test:catalog` covers both. In the browser the same probe was saved with explicit VI/EN slugs (`/slug-tuy-chinh`, `/custom-slug`), then saved again with the VI slug blank: the API derived `/danh-muc-slug-da-doi` from the title, and the probe, its four audit rows and its idempotency key were removed. Changing a live category slug now also writes a 301 for each changed locale (`/categories/<old>` → `/categories/<new>`), through the same auto-slug writer as content. A row a human already wrote is kept, a path that is still live after the save (for example a VI/EN swap) is not redirected, an old path another live category owns is skipped, and reusing an old slug removes the automatic row so the new page is reachable; deleting the category drops the automatic rows that lead to or from its landing, so an older URL answers 404 rather than 301 into a missing page (manual rows stay); `test:catalog` covers those cases. On `sentuan` both old category URLs answered 301 to the new slug and both new URLs answered 200, then the probe, its redirect rows and audit rows were removed. (This paragraph used to say variants and attributes were not editable — that was true of this first cut only; both editors landed the same day, see P4.a (rest) and CAT-02 below.)

### P4.a (rest) — variants and attributes editor (landed 2026-11-10)

- Target files: `tm-cms/server/models/catalog.ts` (two new invariants), `cms-base/app/pages/catalog.vue` (variant/attribute rows in the item modal, payload and validation), `tm-cms/i18n/locales/{vi,en}.json` (`admin.catalog.fields.{variants,addVariant,attributes,…}`, `admin.catalog.errors.{variantRequired,variantSkuRequired}`), `scripts/build/test-catalog-service.ts` (five new checks).
- Behaviour: a variable product now requires at least one variant and a simple product rejects variants, both server-side (`CATALOG_PAYLOAD_INVALID`) and in the modal before the request. Every variant row edits SKU, name, base/sale price, stock, active flag and is re-numbered by the same normalization used for created records; the item attribute list edits name/value. Opening the modal clones the stored variants/attributes, so cancelling never mutates the row still shown in the list, and saving sends them back instead of the empty arrays the first version submitted.
- Evidence (service, disposable DB): `pnpm test:catalog` → 23 checks (five new): variable without variants → `422`, simple with variants → `422`, variants + attributes round-trip through create and list, two variants sharing a SKU (case-insensitive) → `422`, and an update replaces the variant set while keeping attributes and bumping the revision.
- Evidence (browser, `:4005/catalog`, real `dem-bong-ep-vikosan`): the modal lists both existing variants (`SKU-HTFR44J8-7F-120X190`, `SKU-HTFR44J8-7F-150X190`) and the "Biến thể"/"Thuộc tính" sections; saving without edits returned "Đã lưu sản phẩm", and MongoDB still holds 2 variants with the same SKUs at revision 2 — the previous build would have wiped them.
- Evidence (browser, `:4005/catalog`, 2026-11-16 re-check on a throwaway probe, and the list-row fix the same day: `bo-hn-bo1` renders `HN-BO1 · Price 1.050.000 – 2.500.000`, a probe cloned with an empty item SKU and three variants renders `3 variants · Price 100.000 – 300.000` in EN and `3 biến thể · Giá 100.000 – 300.000` in VI, both probes removed; the sale-price pass the same day: a simple probe with `basePrice 110000 / salePrice 80000` renders `Giá 80.000` (not 110.000) and a variable probe whose variants are `100000/60000` and `200000/null` renders `Giá 60.000 – 200.000` (the cheap end discounted, the dear end not), probes removed): a new variable product saved with two variant rows (`PROBE-V1` 100000, `PROBE-V2` 150000) came back as `type: variable` with both variants; reopening the modal showed both rows filled, editing V1 to 120000 and adding `PROBE-V3` (90000) saved at revision 2 with three variants; a third pass removed V3 and added an attribute (`Size = M`) and saved at revision 3 with two variants and `attributes: [{ name: { vi: 'Size' }, value: { vi: 'M' } }]`. The probe, its audit rows and its SKU rows were then removed (`catalog_items` back to 241, audit to 1).
- Limits: product-level attributes edit as `name`/`value` pairs, but a *variant* carries only SKU, name, base/sale price, stock and the active flag — per-variant options (size/colour attached to the variant) are still not part of the model; there is no bulk variant generation, and a variant SKU is required (a legacy variant without one is skipped by the migration). A variable product's list row shows the item SKU when it has one, otherwise `N biến thể`, and the price line becomes a range over what a customer would pay (`Giá 100.000 – 300.000`); with no variants it falls back to the item's own price. That price is the **effective** one — `salePrice` when set, base price otherwise, per variant — read through `getItemPriceRange` in `@tm/flow-utils/price`, which shares `effectivePrice` with the storefront's `getItemDisplayPrice`, so the list cannot quote a different number than the shop; a `salePrice` of 0 counts as no sale, matching the storefront.

### P4.a / CAT-02 — bulk price and stock (landed 2026-11-10)

- Contract: `AdminCatalogBulkItemSchema` (`id`, `revision`, optional `basePrice`/`salePrice`/`stock`), `AdminCatalogBulkPatchSchema` (1–100 rows) and `AdminCatalogBulkResultSchema` (`results[]` with `ok`/`revision`/`error`, plus `succeeded`/`failed`) in `@tm/flow-contracts/catalog-admin`.
- Service/API: `bulkUpdateCatalogItems` in `tm-cms/server/services/catalogService.ts` behind `POST /api/v1/admin/catalog/items/bulk` (`content.write`, same-origin, `no-store`); `POST /api/v1/admin/catalog/items/bulk-by-filter` reuses it with a server-resolved selection from the list filter (deleted rows skipped, `404 CATALOG_BULK_EMPTY` for an empty match, `422 CATALOG_BULK_TOO_LARGE` above 500, shared `buildItemFilter` with the admin list). Each row is revision-checked and audited on its own; a row whose values already match is answered `ok` without a revision bump, and failures (`CATALOG_BULK_DUPLICATE`, `CATALOG_ID_INVALID`, `CATALOG_ITEM_NOT_FOUND`, `CATALOG_REVISION_CONFLICT`, `CATALOG_SALE_PRICE_INVALID`) never roll back the good rows.
- UI: per-row checkboxes, "select all on this page", an "Cập nhật hàng loạt (N đã chọn)" button and a bulk modal (base/sale price, stock; blank keeps the value) with a scope radio — ticked rows, or every record matching the current filter — in `cms-base/app/pages/catalog.vue`; `useCatalogAdmin.bulkUpdate` / `bulkUpdateByFilter`; i18n `admin.catalog.bulk.*`.
- Evidence (service, disposable DB): `pnpm test:catalog` → 31 checks (the seven bulk ones: mixed batch, duplicate id, no-op row keeps revision, unknown id fails alone, filter batch skipping a deleted row, empty filter, filter batch keeping per-row validation, 501 records refused by the cap).
- Evidence (HTTP `:4005`, disposable probes): create `201`, bulk `200` with one good row (`revision 2`) and one `CATALOG_REVISION_CONFLICT`, no-op batch `200` leaving revisions unchanged, `422` for 101 rows, `401` anonymous, `403` foreign Origin. Browser: filtered to the probes, select-all reported `Đã chọn 2`, applying `135000`/stock `7` toasted `Đã cập nhật 2/2 sản phẩm` and the list showed `Giá 135.000` with the selection cleared; MongoDB held `basePrice 135000 / stock 7 / revision 2`. Probes and their audit rows were deleted afterwards (`catalog_items` 241).
- Barcode: `barcode` is a trimmed field on `catalog_items`, unique per site through the partial index `catalog_item_site_barcode` (records without one are excluded), searched by the admin list and edited next to SKU; a collision answers `409 CATALOG_BARCODE_EXISTS` and clearing a value frees it. Evidence: `pnpm test:catalog` → 35 checks (four barcode ones), HTTP `:4005` create/duplicate/update/search/clear/reuse, and the browser duplicate message kept the modal open.
- Limits: price and stock only for bulk (no category/tag/status/variant fields), ticked rows capped at 100 and filter scopes at 500, no undo or progress reporting; barcodes have no format/checksum validation and no label printing; the serial/IMEI screen landed on `/inventory` on 2026-11-22, and the catalog/write import now has a UI, while a serial import screen and CSV export remain open.


### P3.g — legacy-URL redirect map (landed 2026-11-10)

- Contract: `tm-shared/packages/flow-contracts/src/redirect-admin.ts` (`AdminRedirectSchema`, `AdminRedirectDraftSchema`, `AdminRedirectUpdateSchema`, `AdminRedirectListSchema`; status codes 301/302); audit resource type `redirect` added to `ADMIN_AUDIT_RESOURCE_TYPES` and the CMS `AUDIT_RESOURCE_TYPES`.
- Model: `tm-cms/server/models/redirect.ts` writes `content_redirects` (not `redirects`, which holds the legacy `Redirect` documents and stays untouched until the import slice). A row is `siteId`, `from`, `to`, `statusCode`, `isActive`, `note`, `revision`; `content_redirect_site_path_unique` makes one old path unique per site and `content_redirect_active_path` is a partial index for the storefront lookup.
- Service: `tm-cms/server/services/redirectService.ts` — `normalizeRedirectPath` (leading slash, no trailing slash, refuses query/fragment/whitespace/empty segments/absolute URLs), `createRedirect`, `updateRedirect`, `deleteRedirect`, `listRedirects` (literal filter over path/target/note) and `resolveRedirect` (follows at most five hops, reports the first row's status code). Guards: `422 REDIRECT_PATH_INVALID`/`REDIRECT_PATH_REQUIRED`/`REDIRECT_PATH_SAME`, `409 REDIRECT_EXISTS`, `409 REDIRECT_LOOP` (cycle check over the whole active map, applied on create and on update), `409 REDIRECT_REVISION_CONFLICT`, `404 REDIRECT_NOT_FOUND`; every write records one `audit_logs` entry with the actor from the session.
- API: `GET/POST /api/v1/admin/redirects`, `PATCH/DELETE /api/v1/admin/redirects/:id` behind `content.read`/`content.write`, same-origin CSRF check, idempotency keys and `no-store`; mutations ask the storefront to purge its cache (`x-storefront-revalidate`).
- CMS UI: `/redirects` (`cms-base/app/pages/redirects.vue` + `useRedirects`) lists old path → target with status/state badges, a search box, a create/edit modal (paths, 301/302, active switch, note), inline enable/disable and a guarded delete; the nav entry sits with content and is gated by `content.read`. i18n `admin.redirects.*` and `nav.redirects` in both locales, plus the previously missing `admin.audit.resources.{menu,catalog_item,catalog_category,redirect}` labels.
- Storefront: `tm-web/server/middleware/redirects.ts` resolves the request path before routing and answers the stored 301/302; it skips non-GET requests, `/api/`, `/_nuxt`, `__nuxt` and any path with a file extension, and stays inert when the deployment has no Mongo binding. `tm-web/server/utils/publicRedirects.ts` caches the lookup per database for 30s (missing paths included) and `POST /api/_internal/purge` now clears that cache along with the Nitro handler cache, so a configured revalidation makes a map change immediate.
- Evidence (service, disposable DB): `pnpm test:redirects` → 12 checks — normalization and refusals, create with revision 1, duplicate path, self-target, loop refused on create and update, chain resolution keeping the first status code, inactive row ignored then re-enabled, stale revision leaving the row intact, per-site maps with the same paths, literal list filter, revision-guarded delete, one audit entry per write.
- Evidence (HTTP `:4005` + Web `:4006`, disposable probes): anonymous list `401`, foreign Origin `403`; create with `"  /probe-cu/  "` → `201` storing `/probe-cu`; duplicate `409 REDIRECT_EXISTS`; `probe-khong-slash` `422 REDIRECT_PATH_INVALID`; closing a loop `409 REDIRECT_LOOP`; stale revision `409 REDIRECT_REVISION_CONFLICT`; `GET :4006/probe-cu` → `301 Location: /products` while an unmapped path stayed `404`; disabling the row stopped the redirect after the 30s cache window (no local `REVALIDATE_SECRET`, so the purge path answered `503` as designed); `/api/public/site`, `/sitemap.xml`, `/products`, `/bai-viet` still `200`.
- Evidence (browser `:4005/redirects`): the page renders with the "Chuyển hướng" nav entry and "Chuyển hướng URL" heading, the create modal saved `/probe-ui-cu → /products` (row appeared, `3 chuyển hướng`), the row toggle toasted "Đã tắt chuyển hướng" and showed "Đang tắt", and the delete dialog ("Xoá chuyển hướng từ /probe-ui-cu?") removed the row with "Đã xoá chuyển hướng". Probe rows and their audit entries were deleted afterwards (`content_redirects` 0, `audit_logs` back to 1).
- Limits: the map is explicit apart from slug changes (the record below), where the write that renames a published record leaves a `/<old slug>` → `/<new slug>` 301; paths are matched exactly, with no wildcard/prefix or regex rows; a row can also answer 410 instead of redirecting, there is no CSV import of a legacy map, and no purge of an external CDN. A disabled or deleted row stops applying at most 30s later unless the storefront purge is configured.

### CNT-01 / CNT-02 — content keywords record (landed 2026-11-10)

- Contract: `AdminContentLocaleSchema` in `@tm/flow-contracts/content-admin` and `PublicContentSchema` in `@tm/flow-contracts/content-public` both carry `keywords: string[]`; the public schema requires the field, so a reader that forgets it fails loudly instead of shipping a page without keywords.
- Model: `tm-cms/server/models/content.ts` stores `keywords` inside each locale (`KEYWORDS_MAX_COUNT = 20`, `KEYWORD_MAX_LENGTH = 60`), trims and de-duplicates input, drops blank entries, and returns `keywords` in `toPublicContent`. `tm-cms/server/models/revision.ts` gained the same field in its snapshot subschema: a snapshot is what a restore writes back, so omitting it there would have dropped keywords on every restore.
- Commands: no new route — the existing create/update/event paths accept the field, `contentFields` includes it in every revision snapshot and diff, and the SEO image the modal does not edit is now carried through instead of being reset to an empty string on save.
- CMS UI: the locale block in `content.vue` has a "Từ khóa" input (comma separated, hint states the 20-keyword cap), loaded from the record and sent back with the locale; i18n `admin.content.fields.keywords{,Hint,Placeholder}` in both locales.
- Storefront: `tm-web/server/models/contentPublic.ts` maps the field inside its allowlist and `[...slug].vue` renders `<meta name="keywords">` only when the list is non-empty, so pages without keywords keep no empty tag.
- Evidence (service, disposable DB): `pnpm test:content` → 25 model checks and 30 live service checks, including `[' Trang sức ', 'trang sức', '  ', 'Quà tặng']` stored as `['Trang sức', 'trang sức', 'Quà tặng']`, the public read returning the same list, 21 keywords and a 61-character keyword both `422 CONTENT_PAYLOAD_INVALID`, a restore bringing the snapshot's keywords back, and the allowlist checks naming `keywords`.
- Evidence (HTTP, disposable probe on `:4005`/`:4006`): create with padded/duplicate keywords → `201` storing three values; publish → `200`; `GET /api/public/content/pages/probe-keywords` returned `["Trang sức","trang sức","Quà tặng"]` and `GET /probe-keywords` rendered `content="Trang sức, trang sức, Quà tặng"`; `PATCH` with `["quà tặng","trang sức","trang sức"]` stored two and the rendered tag followed; `GET /gioi-thieu` (no keywords) carried no keywords tag; a revision detail showed the snapshot's keywords; a page with no keywords stayed `200`.
- Evidence (browser `:4005/content`): the edit modal shows "Từ khóa" filled with `quà tặng, trang sức`, saving "quà tặng, trang sức, trang sức bạc" produced revision 5 with those three keywords, kept `seo.image`, and the storefront tag updated; the probe record, its five audit rows and five revisions were deleted afterwards (`content` back to 3 documents, `audit_logs` 1).
- Limits: keywords are free text per locale with no global `keywords` collection, no tag-style rename/merge and no automatic sync from a legacy keyword document; the admin list filters by exact keyword and storefront search matches them literally (next two records). Tags remain the taxonomy; keywords are only SEO metadata.

### CNT-02 — content attributes record (landed 2026-11-10)

- Model/contract unchanged: `attributes: Record<string, string | number | boolean>` was already stored, validated against `^[a-zA-Z][a-zA-Z0-9_.-]{0,59}$` (values string ≤500 / number / boolean) and audited through `contentFields` and revision snapshots. What was missing was a way to edit it: the modal submitted `attributes: {}` on every save, so opening a record and pressing Save erased whatever the API had stored.
- CMS UI: the record modal has an "Thuộc tính" field with one key/value row per attribute, add ("Thêm thuộc tính") and remove buttons. Keys are validated before the request (`attributeKeyRequired`, `attributeKeyInvalid`, `attributeDuplicate`), rows that are empty on both sides are dropped, and a row whose text still matches the stored value is sent back with its original type, so `count: 5` stays a number and `featured: true` stays a boolean instead of becoming strings. i18n `admin.content.fields.{attributes,attributesHint,attributeKey,attributeValue,addAttribute,removeAttribute}` and `admin.content.errors.{attributeKeyRequired,attributeKeyInvalid,attributeDuplicate}` in both locales.
- Evidence (service, disposable DB): `pnpm test:content` → 25 model + 30 live service checks, now asserting attributes round-trip (`{ layout: 'wide' }`) and that an unsafe key (`1bad key`) is `422 CONTENT_PAYLOAD_INVALID`.
- Evidence (browser `:4005/content`, disposable probe `pages/probe-attributes` with `{ layout: 'wide', count: 5, featured: true }`): the edit modal loaded three rows with those values; changing the first key to `9bad` and saving showed "Khóa thuộc tính phải bắt đầu bằng chữ…" and stayed open, with no request; after restoring `layout` and adding `theme: gold`, saving produced revision 2 with `{ layout: 'wide', count: 5, featured: true, theme: 'gold' }` and the stored types still number/boolean. The probe record, its two audit rows and two revisions were deleted afterwards (`content` back to 3 documents, `audit_logs` 1).
- Limits: flat key/value strings only — no nested attributes, no typed editor (a new value is always a string), no attribute filter or public projection of attributes, and no schema per content type.

### CNT-01 — keyword filter on the content list (landed 2026-11-10)

- Service: `listContent` accepts `keyword`, trimmed and capped at the model's 60-character limit, matched exactly against `locales.keywords` so a value on any locale (not only the active one) selects the record. The free-text `q` search also covers `locales.keywords` next to key, title and slug.
- API/contract: `ContentListQuerySchema` gained `keyword` (trimmed, 1–60, optional; a whitespace-only value is `422 CONTENT_QUERY_INVALID` like the other filters), and `ContentListQuery` in `useContentAdmin` mirrors it.
- CMS UI: the content toolbar has a keyword input next to the tag filter, debounced 400 ms like the search box; the search placeholder now names keywords too. i18n `admin.content.keywordFilter` in both locales.
- Evidence (service, disposable DB): `pnpm test:content` → 31 live service checks, including an exact hit, a case-mismatched value returning nothing (keywords are not folded), an `en`-locale keyword matching through the dotted path, a padded value still matching, another site's identical keyword excluded, and `q` finding a record by keyword alone.
- Evidence (HTTP `:4005`, disposable probes `pages/probe-kw-alpha`/`-beta`): `?keyword=probe-kw-alpha` returned only alpha, `?keyword=%20probe-kw-beta%20` returned only beta, `?keyword=PROBE-KW-ALPHA` returned none, the shared keyword `dung-chung` returned both, `?q=probe-kw-beta` returned beta via its keyword, and a whitespace-only keyword was `422`.
- Evidence (browser `:4005/content`): typing `probe-kw-alpha` in the new field left only the alpha row while the site-wide stat stayed at 5; clearing the field restored all rows. The two probe records and their audit/revision rows were deleted afterwards (`content` 3, `audit_logs` 1).
- Limits: exact match only (no partial/folded matching), one keyword at a time, and no keyword facet with counts (unlike tags, keywords have no list endpoint). The storefront search matches keywords literally since the next record.

### WEB-04 — keywords reach storefront search (landed 2026-11-10)

- Query: `buildPublishedListFilter` adds `locales.keywords` to the `$or` behind `q`, still as a bounded, escaped, case-insensitive literal regex. Because keywords live inside a locale entry, the condition is an `$elemMatch` on `locales` limited to the requested locale plus its fallback, so a keyword written only for `en` cannot surface the record to a `vi` listing.
- Behaviour: a page whose title, slug and excerpt share nothing with its keyword is now found by that keyword alone; title/slug/excerpt/key search is unchanged, and the published/window/locale policy still applies, so a keyword on a draft stays private. No new route or contract field: `/api/public/content?q=` and `/tim-kiem` inherit it.
- Evidence (service, disposable DB): `pnpm test:web-content` → 20 live reader checks, the new one asserting a `vi` keyword hit (`nhan-hieu-ben-vung`, also in upper case), an `en`-only keyword returning nothing for `vi` and the record for `en`, and a draft keyword returning nothing.
- Evidence (HTTP `:4006`, probe page `pages/probe-public-kw` published with keywords `probe-public-kw`/`nhãn-hiệu-probe`): `?q=probe-public-kw` and `?q=PROBE-PUBLIC-KW` each returned the key, `?q=nhãn-hiệu-probe` matched through the accented keyword only, `?q=Public kw` still matched the title, `?q=.*` stayed literal (no rows) and an unknown term returned none.
- Evidence (browser `:4006/tim-kiem`): `?q=nh%C3%A3n-hi%E1%BB%87u-probe` rendered the heading for that term with the `Probe public kw` card, proving the page finds a record by keyword alone. The probe record, its 3 audit rows and 3 revisions were deleted afterwards (`content` 3, `audit_logs` 1).
- Limits: matching is a raw regex on the stored keyword, so accents and case-insensitivity behave as Mongo's `i` flag does but there is no diacritic folding (`nhan-hieu-probe` does not find `nhãn-hiệu-probe`), no phrase/synonym handling, and keyword matches are not highlighted differently from title matches on the search page.

### WEB-20 — a gate for query-param mismatches between links and their pages (landed 2026-11-13)

- `test:web-routes` now also scans navigation links that carry a `query: { ... }` object and checks that every key in it is a key the target page reads from `route.query`. A page that reads `route.query[key]` dynamically is treated as accepting any key, so `/bai-viet` and `/tim-kiem` stay out of the check.
- This is the class of bug that made `tim-kiem.vue`'s "view all products" link pass `search=` while `products/index.vue` read `q` — the filter was silently dropped and the link landed on the unfiltered list. The gate now fails with `/products: query key "search" is never read by the target page`.
- Proven both ways: reverting the link to `query: { search: q }` made the gate exit `1` naming the key and the target; restoring `query: { q }` put it back to green. `verify:all` is 28/28.
- Limits: only links written as `{ path, query: { ... } }` (or the reverse order) are seen; a query built at runtime or passed through a variable is out of scope, same as the path gate.

### WEB-19 — the dead-link gate now covers the CMS too (landed 2026-11-13)

- The same gate that caught `/about` and `/contact` now reads both app roots: the storefront and the CMS. `scripts/build/test-web-routes.ts` takes an `APPS` list (root, page directory, minimum link count) and runs the same manifest + link scan + resolution per app, so a new sidebar entry in `cms-base/layouts/default.vue` that points at a page which was renamed or removed fails `verify:all` instead of waiting for a click.
- Proven both ways: moving `cms-base/app/pages/audit.vue` out made the gate exit `1` naming `/audit (…/pages/index.vue:176)`, and restoring it put it back to green. `verify:all` is 28/28.
- Limits unchanged: literal targets only, and the catch-all page still does not count as a route for either app.

### WEB-18 — product names are highlighted, and a query-param bug in the search page (landed 2026-11-13)

- `CatalogProductCard` gains an optional `term` prop; when it is set the name and the summary render through the existing `WebSearchHighlight` instead of plain text, so the visitor's term is wrapped in `<mark>`. `tim-kiem.vue` passes `q` and `products/index.vue` passes `listQuery.search`, so the highlight only ever reflects a query that was actually searched.
- The same page had a real bug hidden behind the same code path: `tim-kiem.vue`'s "view all products" link was `query: { search: q }`, but `products/index.vue` reads `route.query.q` (its `search` field is derived from `q`). The link landed on the unfiltered list. It now passes `q`.
- Evidence (`sentuan`, built into `tm-web/.output`, preview on `:4018`, `q=Bo`): `/tim-kiem` renders 16 `<mark>` across 8 product cards, the view-all link is `/products?q=Bo`, and `/products?q=Bo` renders 24 `<mark>` on the same names. `pnpm typecheck` 0, `pnpm lint` 0, `pnpm test:web-routes` 4/4, `pnpm verify:all` 28/28.
- Limits: only literal terms are highlighted and only in the card title and summary; the term is escaped before it becomes a pattern, so it cannot inject markup. The `CatalogProductCard`'s category and price are deliberately left plain.

### WEB-17 — a dead-link gate, and a second look at WEB-16 (landed 2026-11-13)

- The review found the two pages rendering the same thing twice: `/about` printed exactly the email/phone/address block `/contact` owns, and the empty state was copy-pasted. `/about` now shows only the customer's description plus a link to `/contact`, and both pages render the shared `WebNotice` (the same shape as the existing `CatalogStateNotice`), so the layer has one empty state instead of three. `contact.vue` also stopped modelling "no link" as `href: ''` — the address row simply has no `href` — and the four about-only message keys left with the block they described.
- What let the bug through: a link to a page that does not exist typechecks, lints and builds. `pnpm test:web-routes` (`scripts/build/test-web-routes.ts`, part of the default `verify:all` set) reads the page files, scans the layer's literal internal targets (`to=`, `to:`, `href=`, `navigateTo`, `router.push`) and fails with `target (file:line)`.
- The catch-all page deliberately does not satisfy a link: it renders CMS content by path, which is exactly how `/about` answered `404` while still "having a page". Without that rule the gate would pass on any href and be worthless.
- Proven both ways: delete `about.vue` and the gate exits `1`, naming `/about has no page file` and `links point at pages that do not exist: /contact (tm-shared/nuxt-layers/web-base/app/pages/about.vue:45)`; restore it and it is green again, with `pnpm verify:all` at 28/28.
- Not covered: literal targets only. A link built at runtime (`/products/${slug}`) is out of scope, and so is a CMS-managed menu row — those hrefs are admin content, not routes in the repository.

### WEB-16 — the header's `/about` and `/contact` links pointed at nothing (landed 2026-11-13)

- Found in the per-customer browser pass, not by a gate: on the production build of `sentuan`, `/products`, `/bai-viet`, `/danh-muc` and a product detail answered `200`, while `/about` and `/contact` answered `404` — although both the header fallback menu (`usePublicSite`) and the footer (`layouts/default.vue`) link to them, and the web layer has no such pages.
- Fix: two pages in `web-base`, both reading the site's own settings through `usePublicSite()`/`useSiteConfig()` — a phone number or an address is never invented, so a site that has saved none gets an explicit empty state instead of a form that goes nowhere. `usePageRobots({ empty })` keeps a contentless page out of the index.
- State of this site (as the DB actually is): settings company fields are all empty, so `/about` renders "This page has no content yet" and `/contact` "No contact details yet", both `noindex, follow`. The moment an operator fills the company block in the CMS, the same pages render the real details with no code change.
- Evidence: after `TM_CUSTOMER_ID=sentuan pnpm build:web` and a preview on `:4018`, `/about` and `/contact` answer `200` (`<title>Giới thiệu Sen Tuấn Jewelry & Gemstones</title>`, `<h1>` matching), the main area shows the empty state with its panel, and `robots` is `noindex, follow`. `pnpm typecheck` and `pnpm lint` clean, `pnpm verify:all` 27/27.
- Not covered: the same check was not repeated for every customer, and no test asserts the two routes exist — a scaffold check would be the next step if these pages are expected to stay.

### WEB-15 — `@tuanmjnh/tm-ui` adopted through the link (landed 2026-11-13)

- What landed: the CMS module badges (`ModuleStatusBadges.vue`, `/inventory`, `/orders`, `/catalog`, `/reports`, `/ai`) draw the package's `StatusBadge` instead of two hand-written `UBadge`s, with `@tuanmjnh/tm-ui` linked into the `cms-base` layer.
- The four wiring steps are in `tm-platform/docs/upgrade.md` §0: the dependency (in the layer that imports it), `vite.server.fs.allow`, `@source` for the package's `dist`, and a client plugin registering the primitives the package resolves (`UBadge`, `UButton`, `UIcon`, `UPopover`, `USelectMenu`, `UTooltip` — the union from `grep resolveComponent dist/*.js`, not just the ones the badge needs).
- What unblocked WEB-13: the fourth requirement, one Vue instance. `vue` is not reachable from any `node_modules` here, so `vite.resolve.dedupe` has nothing to resolve from the app root, and adding `vue` as a dependency to make it work broke `vue-tsc` with 122 errors. Instead `tm-cms/nuxt.config.ts` aliases `vue` (exact match) to the build the app already loads, found in the pnpm store (`node_modules/.pnpm/vue@*/node_modules/vue/dist/vue.runtime.esm-bundler.js`). No dependency changes, typecheck stays green, and the package stops resolving its own 3.5.45 next to our 3.5.44.
- Evidence: on the dev CMS `/inventory` the pills are `span[data-slot="base"]` carrying `bg-success/10 text-success ring ring-inset ring-success/25` and `rounded-[4px]`, with `document.querySelectorAll('ubadge').length` at `0` — the same class set measured in the WEB-13 probe at 6.44:1 / 5.85:1 (light) and 7.95:1 / 9.03:1 (dark). `active:scale-95`, a class that exists only in the package's dist, is present in the built CSS, so the `@source` scan really runs. `pnpm typecheck` 0 errors, `TM_CUSTOMER_ID=sentuan pnpm build:cms` exit 0, `pnpm verify:all` 27/27.
- Not verified: the model picker (`ModelSelect`) has no call site in this repo, so its four extra primitives are registered but unexercised; the alias is a dev-and-build resolution rule, so a Vue minor bump in `tm-platform` no longer reaches this app — which is the intended trade and is why publishing the package (peer `vue`) is still the right end state.
- The alias path is read from the pnpm store by directory name (`vue@*`). It is not a dependency contract, and a second `vue@` in the store would make the first match arbitrary; if that ever happens, replace it with an explicit dependency and `dedupe`, and re-run the typecheck before believing it.

### WEB-14 — shared envelope from `tm-platform`, attempted and reverted (2026-11-13)

- What was tried: `@tuanmjnh/tm-types@0.1.0` installed from the npm registry into `cms-base`, and `useCategoryAdmin`'s list response typed as `Omit<ApiResponse<AdminCategory[]>, 'data'> & { data, tree }`.
- Why it was reverted on review: `ApiResponse` only contributes `success`, `error?` and `meta?`, and this client reads none of them; its optional `data` contradicts an endpoint that always answers with a payload, and the `Omit` that repairs that is longer than the four-line interface it replaced while hiding the guarantee behind two type operators. The other candidate in the same file, `ApiErrorBody`, is not a better home either: it models Nitro's three nesting levels (`data.error.code`, `data.data.error.code`, `error.code`) where the shared type could only stand in for the innermost `code`, so it would keep the wrapper union and add a `Pick`/`NonNullable` chain.
- Consequence: no dependency, no `minimumReleaseAgeExclude` line, no lockfile entry; `useCategoryAdmin.ts` is identical to its pre-adoption state and `pnpm verify:all` is 27/27. What the attempt did prove is the registry route itself — install, supply-chain gate, resolution — which `link:` could not offer, and that is the finding worth keeping.
- Not adopted: `tm-utils` is Hub-shaped (`deviceCredentialScope`, `parseUpstreamAiError`) and `tm-validators`' `PaginationParamsSchema` assumes page/limit offsets while every list endpoint here is cursor-based.

### WEB-13 — shared packages from `tm-platform` (probe, blocked 2026-11-13)
